(1)      Net profit margin is profit for the year from continuing operations divided by     (1)   淨利潤率指持續經營業務年內溢利除以持續經
         revenue from continuing operations.                                                      營業務收入。
(2)      Return on total assets is profit for the year from continuing operations divided   (2)   總資產回報率指持續經營業務年內溢利除以持
         by average total assets from continuing operations.                                      續經營業務平均資產總額。
(3)      Return on capital employed is the ratio of profit before income tax expense        (3)   淨資本回報率指持續經營業務除所得稅費用及
         and interest from continuing operations divided by total assets less current             利息前溢利除以持續經營業務總資產減流動負
         liabilities from continuing operations.                                                  債。
(4)      Liabilities-to EBITDA ratio is the ratio of total liabilities from continuing      (4)   負債對EBITDA倍數指持續經營業務總負債除以
         operations divided by EBITDA.                                                            利息、稅項、折舊及攤銷前盈利。
(5)      Debts-to-net assets ratio is the ratio of the sum of interest-bearing borrowings   (5)   淨資產負債率指計息借貸與租賃負債之和除以
         and lease liabilities divided by total equity.                                           總權益。
(6)      Gearing ratio is the ratio of the sum of interest-bearing borrowings, and lease    (6)   資本負債率指計息借貸與租賃負債之和除以總
         liabilities divided by the sum of total equity, interest-bearing borrowings and          權益、計息借貸與租賃負債之和。
         lease liability.
(7)      Debts-to-assets ratio is the ratio of the sum of interest-bearing borrowings       (7)   資產負債率指計息借貸與租賃負債之和除以總
         and lease liabilities divided by total assets.                                           資產。
(8)      Average finance cost is the ratio of aggregated amount of each category            (8)   平均融資成本指各種類借貸成本乘其佔持續經
         of borrowing multiply by its interest rate divided by the total borrowing from           營業務總借貸比例，然後加總。
         continuing operations.

                                     昆侖能源有限公司 二零二三年年度報告   11
              CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
              主席報告




Respected shareholders:                                                    尊敬的各位股東：
In 2023, while the global energy industry has gradually shaken off the     2023年，全球能源產業逐漸擺脫世紀疫情
haze of the once-in-a-century pandemic and geopolitical conflicts, the     和地緣衝突陰霾，天然氣需求回歸正增長軌
demand for natural gas returned to the track of positive growth. The       道。國內宏觀政策效果持續釋放，天然氣消
effect of domestic macro policies continued to be released, and natural    費恢復向好，全年表觀消費量3,945.3億立方
gas consumption recovered. The annual apparent consumption of the          米，同比增長7.6%。本集團秉持「助力美麗中
year amounted to 394,530 million cubic meters, representing a year-        國建設，點靚人民美好生活」企業願景，更加
on-year increase of 7.6%. By adhering to the corporate mission of          注重價值創造，健全完善治理體系，著力提
“Beautiful China of empower construction, brighten wonderful life of       升發展質量，主營業務量效齊增、新興業務
people”, the Group paid more attention to value creation, improved the     穩健佈局、資本市場表現強勁，高質量發展
governance system, and strove to improve the quality of development.
With increases in both the volume and efficiency of its primary business
and the steady layout of emerging businesses, the Group achieved

                                                                                         主席報告




strong performance in the capital markets, the high-quality development   再上新台階，在國資委「雙百企業」考核中躍升
has reached a new high, enabling the Group to be recognised as a          為「標桿」企業。
“benchmark enterprise” in the SASAC’s” Double-Hundred Enterprises”
assessment.

Overview of the Company’s performance                                     公司業績概述

                                                                                       昆侖能源有限公司 二零二三年年度報告   19
             MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS
             管理層討論與分析


During the year, following the normalisation of operation of the economy and society of China,
the upward trending in economy brought the demand of gas consumption in commerce and
industry, power generation and other segment. The Group grasped the beneficial timing when
the market rebounded to cope with multiple challenges such as the large fluctuation in market
and increasing cost of resources, continue to optimise its resource allocation and innovative
marketing strategies and methods in order to realise the continuous growth in main business.
Profit before income tax expense was approximately RMB12,593 million, representing an
increase of 10.5% as compared with RMB11,392 million for the last year. Profit attributable to
shareholders of the Company for the Year was approximately RMB5,682 million, representing
an increase of 8.7% as compared with RMB5,228 million for the last year. Core profit attributable
to shareholders of the Company for the Year was approximately RMB6,144 million, representing
a decrease of 2.2% as compared with RMB6,281 million for the last year.
本年度，隨著中國經濟社會恢復常態化運行，經濟形勢回升向好，帶動工商業、發
電等用氣需求增長，本集團緊抓市場回暖的有利時機，克服市場波動大、資源成本
上升等多重挑戰，持續優化資源配置，精益營銷，實現了主營業務持續增長。各主
要業務板塊均取得了良好業績，實現之除所得稅費用前溢利約為人民幣12,593百萬
元，比去年人民幣11,392百萬元增加10.5%。於本年度本公司股東應佔溢利約為人
民幣5,682百萬元，比去年人民幣5,228百萬元增加8.7%。股東應佔核心溢利人民幣
6,144百萬元，比去年人民幣6,281百萬元減少2.2%。

Dividend Policy                                                           股息政策
The dividend policy of the Company allows shareholders to share the       本公司之股息政策允許股東分享本公司的利
Company’s profits and also enables the Company to retain sufficient       潤，同時亦可為本公司的未來發展保留足夠
reserves for its future development. Under the circumstances that         的儲備。在本公司盈利且不影響本集團正常
the Company makes profits and has no effect on the Group’s normal         經營的情況下，本公司擬以年度股息形式與
business operation, the Company intends to share its profits with         股東分享溢利，每年派息總額不少於本公司
its shareholders in the form of annual dividend, which shall be in a      股東應佔本集團合併年度溢利之25%，惟需
total distribution amount of not less than 25% of the Group’s annual      受以下「考慮因素」所限。
consolidated profit attributable to the shareholders of the Company, on
the condition that it shall be subject to the following “Criteria”.

      surplus, contractual restrictions, actual and expected national          制、實際及預期的國家財政狀況、宏
      financial conditions, macroeconomic conditions and other factors         觀經濟狀況及董事會認為有關之其他因
      as the Board deems relevant.                                             素。

      criterion and the Company will not assure the distribution of            有所差異，本公司不會確保在任何特定

Practices and Conduct of Meetings                                               會議常規及操守

Notices of regular Board meetings are served to all Directors at least 14       董事會定期會議通知至少於會議前14日向全
days before the meetings and reasonable notice is generally given for           體董事發出，而其他董事會會議一般會發出
other Board meetings. For committee meetings, notices are served in             合理通知。委員會會議將根據有關職權範圍
accordance with the required notice period stated in the relevant terms         所訂之所須通知期發出通知。
of reference.

Agenda and Board papers together with all appropriate, complete                 議程及董事會文件連同所有適當、完備及可
and reliable information are normally sent to all Directors/committee           靠資料一般至少於各董事會會議或委員會會
members at least 3 days before each Board meeting or committee                  議舉行前三天送交全體董事╱委員會成員，
meeting to keep the Directors abreast of the latest developments and            以令董事知悉本公司之最近發展及財政狀
financial position of the Company and to enable them to make informed           況，並能夠在掌握有關資料之情況下作出決
decisions. The Board and each Director also have separate and                   定。需要時，董事會及每名董事亦有個別及
independent access to the senior management whenever necessary.                 獨立途徑接觸高級管理人員。

The Chairman, Chief Executive Officer, Chief Financial Officer and              需要時，主席、行政總裁、財務總監及公司
Company Secretary will attend Board and committee meetings,                     秘書將出席董事會及委員會會議，以就本
when necessary, to advise on business developments, financial and               公司之業務發展、財務及會計事宜、法規遵
accounting matters, statutory compliance, corporate governance and              守、企業管治及其他重要方面提供意見。
other major aspects of the Company.

Minutes of all Board meetings and committee meetings are kept by the            所有董事會會議及委員會會議之會議紀要由
Company Secretary. Minutes are prepared after each meeting and the              公司秘書備存。會議紀要於各會議後編製，
final version is signed by the Chairman or the chairman of the relevant         而最後定稿則由主席或相關委員會主席（視情
committees (as the case may be) and confirmed by the Board in the               況而定）簽署，並於下一次董事會會議由董事
following Board Meeting or by the relevant committee in the following           會或於下一次委員會會議由相關委員會（視情
committee meeting (as the case may be). The confirmed minutes are               況而定）確認。經確認之會議紀要乃備存供日
kept for future reference and Directors’ inspection.                            後參照及董事查閱之用。

involves a conflict of interests for a substantial shareholder or a Director,   董事之利益衝突之重大交易，將由董事會於
will be considered and dealt with by the Board at a duly convened               正式召開之董事會會議上作出考慮及處理。
Board meeting. The Company’s Bye-laws also contain provisions                   本公司細則亦載有條文，規定董事於批准該

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROLS                                      風險管理及內部監控
It is the responsibility of the Board to ensure that the Company           董事會負責確保本公司之內部監控系統穩健
maintains sound and effective internal controls to safeguard the           妥善而且有效，以保障股東之投資及本公司
shareholders’ investment and the Company’s assets. The internal            之資產。本公司之內部監控系統包含完善之
control system of the Company comprises a well-established                 公司架構以及全面之政策及標準。本公司亦
organisational structure and comprehensive policies and standards. The     設有內部審計部門，負責監控本集團內部管
Company also has an internal audit department, which is responsible        治及監督本集團風險管理及內部控制系統。
for monitoring the internal governance of the Group and overseeing the
risk management and internal control system of the Group.

The Board, through the Audit Committee, assesses annually the              董事會透過審核委員會每年評核本集團內部
effectiveness of the Group’s internal control system which covers          監控系統之效能。該系統涵蓋所有重要監
all material controls, including financial, operational and compliance     控，包括本公司的財務、營運及遵守法規的
controls as well as risk management of the Company. The Board              監控以及風險管理。董事會明白，設計有關
acknowledges that such risk management and internal control systems        風險管理及內部控制系統的原意乃管理而非
are designed to manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to        排除未能達成業務目標的風險，並只能就重
achieve business objectives, and can only provide reasonable and           大錯誤陳述或損失提供合理而非絕對的保
not absolute assurance against material misstatement or loss. Having       證。經審閱外部諮詢公司發行的評估報告
reviewed the assessment report issued by an external advisory firm, the    後，董事認為，本集團風險管理及內部控制
Board considers that the risk management and internal control systems      系統有效及充足。
of the Group are effective and adequate.

The Group has been attaching great importance to internal control          本集團歷來高度重視內控與風險管理工作，
and risk management. The Company continued to facilitate the               本公司不斷推進內控體系建設，逐步建立和
building of its internal control system, gradually built and improved a    完善全面風險管理體系，有效促進了依法合
comprehensive risk management system, and effectively promoted             規經營。積極促進風險管理與業務相融合，
its operation in compliance with the laws and regulations. It actively     充分發揮風險在管理中的導向性作用。
pushed forward the integration of risk management and business and
gave full play to the guiding role of risks in its management.

        (a)   Foreign exchange rate risk                                       (a)   外匯風險

              The Group is exposed to foreign exchange rate risk                     本集團承受的外匯風險主要
              primarily through sales and purchases which give                       來自產生以外幣（即與交易
              rise to receivables, payables and cash balances that                   有關的業務所涉及的功能貨
              are denominated in foreign currencies, i.e. currencies                 幣以外的貨幣）計值的應收
              other than the functional currencies of the operations                 款項、應付款項及現金結
              to which the transactions relate. The currency giving                  餘的買賣交易。引致此項
              rise to this risk is primarily United States dollar (“US               風險的貨幣主要為美元（「美
              dollar”).                                                              元」
                                                                                      ）。

              denominated in a currency other than the functional                    能貨幣以外之貨幣計值之已
              currency of the entity to which they relate. For                       確認資產或負債而產生。就
              presentation purposes, the amounts of the exposure                     呈報目的而言，風險承擔額

              excluded.                                                              括在內。

        (b) Credit risk                                                                   (b) 信貸風險

                                                                                                  收賬款。

              As a majority of the Group’s cash at bank and time                                  本集團大部分銀行現金及定

              and financial institutions within the CNPC Group,                                   行及中油集團內的金融機
              management believes that the credit risk is low.                                    構，管理層認為信貸風險較

                  another financial asset.                                                   責任之風險。

                  The Group’s liquidity risk management involves                             本集團之流動資金風險管理
                  maintaining sufficient cash and cash equivalents and                       涉及維持充足現金及現金等
                  availability of funding through an adequate amount                         值項目，以及透過適當之授
                  of committed credit facilities. Management prepares                        信額度提供可用資金。管理
                  monthly cash flow budget to ensure that the Group                          層每月編製現金流量預算以
                  will always have sufficient liquidity to meet its financial                確保本集團擁有足夠的流動
                  obligations as they fall due. The Group arranges and                       資金履行到期財務義務。本
                  negotiates financing with financial institutions and                       集團安排並與金融機構進行
                  maintains a certain level of standby credit facilities to                  融資磋商，保持一定水平的
                  reduce the Group’s liquidity risk.                                         備用授信額度以降低本集團
                                                                                             的流動資金風險。

                  Based on the existing level of gearing and continued                       鑒於目前資本負債率水平以
                  access to funding, the Group believes that its liquidity                   及持續獲得融資，本集團相
                  risk is not material.                                                      信其流動資金風險並不高。

                    identical assets or liabilities at the measurement                    債於活躍市場之未經
                    date.                                                                 調整報價計量之公平
                                                                                          值。

              –     Level 2 valuations: Fair value measured using                    –    第二級估值：使用估
                    valuation techniques which maximise the use                           值技術計量之公平
                    of observable market data and rely as little                          值，該等估值技術盡
                    as possible on entity-specific estimates. If                          量利用可觀察市場數
                    all significant inputs required to fair value an                      據而極少依賴實體的
                    instrument are observable, the instrument is                          特定估計。倘工具的
                    included in level 2.                                                  公平值所需所有重大
                                                                                          輸入數據均為可觀察
                                                                                          數據，則該工具計入
                                                                                          第二級。

              –     Level 3 valuations: Fair value measured using                    –    第三級估值：使用重
                    significant unobservable inputs.                                      大不可觀察輸入數據
                                                                                          計量之公平值。

4     CRITICAL ACCOUNTING JUDGEMENTS AND                                   4   關鍵會計判斷及估計
      ESTIMATES
      Accounting judgments and estimates are regularly evaluated               本集團定期根據過往經驗及其他因素
      by the Group, based on historical experience and other factors           （包 括 對 未 來 事 件 在 當 前 情 況 下 認 為
      which include expectations of future events that are believed to         合理之預期）對會計判斷及估計進行評
      be reasonable under the circumstances.                                   估。

      Significant sources of judgments and estimation uncertainty arise        判斷及估計不明朗因素主要來自LNG加
      from the impairment assessment of the LNG processing plants              工廠減值評估，載述如下：
      and are described as follows:

      Property, plant and equipment, including construction in                 物業、廠房及設備（包括在建工程）乃每
      progress, are reviewed for possible impairments whenever events          當事件或情況變化顯示賬面值可能無法
      or changes in circumstances indicate that the carrying amount            收回時予以檢討是否可能減值。確定資
      may not be recoverable. Determination as to whether and how              產是否減值及減值之金額涉及管理層之
      much an asset is impaired involves management estimates and              估計及判斷，例如天然氣之未來價格、
      judgements such as future prices of natural gas, future production       未來生產成本及貼現預計現金流所用之
      costs and discount rates used in discounting the projected               貼現率。然而，減值檢討及計算乃根據
      cash flows. However, the impairment reviews and calculations             與本集團之相關計劃一致之假設而作
      are based on assumptions that are consistent with the Group’s            出。若干假設之有利變動或會令本集團
      relevant budget. Favourable changes to some assumptions may              避免對任何資產進行減值，而不利變動
      allow the Group to avoid the need to impair any assets, whereas          或會使資產減值。有關LNG加工廠風險

5     REVENUE (CONTINUED)                                                  5   收入（續）
      Accounting policy of revenue recognition:                                收入確認會計政策：

      Income is classified by the Group as revenue when it arises from         當收益產生自本集團日常業務過程中之
      the sale of goods, the provision of services or the use by others        貨品銷售、提供之服務或他人使用本集
      of the Group’s assets under leases in the ordinary course of the         團租賃資產，則本集團會將該收益分類
      Group’s business.                                                        為收入。

      Revenue is recognised when control over a product or service             收入在產品或服務之控制權轉讓予客戶
      is transferred to the customer, or the lessee has the right to use       或在承租人有權使用該資產時獲確認，
      the asset, at the amount of promised consideration to which              金額為本集團預期有權收取之承諾代
      the Group is expected to be entitled, excluding those amounts            價，不包括代表第三方收取之有關金
      collected on behalf of third parties. Revenue excludes value             額。收入不包括增值稅或其他銷售稅，
      added tax or other sales taxes and is after deduction of any trade       並扣減任何貿易折扣。
      discounts.

      (i)   Sales of goods                                                     (i)   銷售貨品
            Revenue is recognised when the customer takes                            收入於客戶管有並接納產品時獲
            possession of and accepts the products. If the products                  確認。倘產品屬部分履行涵蓋其
            are a partial fulfilment of a contract covering other goods              他貨品及/或服務之合約，則確認
            and/or services, then the amount of revenue recognised                   之收入金額為合約項下總交易價
            is an appropriate proportion of the total transaction price              之適當比例，按相對獨立售價基
            under the contract, allocated between all the goods and                  準根據合約協定之全部貨品及服
            services promised under the contract on a relative stand-                務之間分配。
            alone selling price basis.

      (ii) Rendering of services                                               (ii) 提供服務
            The Group’s revenue from rendering of services is primarily              本集團提供服務之收入源於提供
            attributable to provision of LNG gasification and unloading              LNG氣化及卸載服務。提供該等
            services. Revenue from the rendering of these services                   服務之收入於完成LNG氣化或卸
            is recognised in the profit or loss upon the gasification or             載後確認。
            unloading of LNG.

33 SEGMENT INFORMATION                                                   33 板塊資料
   Operating segments are reported in a manner consistent with             營運板塊之呈報方式與向主要營運決策
   the internal reporting provided to the chief operating decision         者提供之內部報告貫徹一致，乃由本集
   maker which is determined by the Group’s most senior executive          團最高管理層及本公司執行董事決定。
   management and Executive Directors of the Company.

   The Group organises its business around products and services.          本集團按產品及服務組織其業務。就產
   From the products and services perspective, the Group is                品及服務而言，本集團廣泛從事一系列
   engaged in a broad range of oil and gas related activities and          石油和天然氣相關業務，其收入來自四
   derives its revenue from its four operating segments: Natural           個營運板塊：天然氣銷售、LPG銷售、
   Gas Sales, Sales of LPG, LNG Processing and Terminal and                LNG加工與儲運及勘探與生產。
   Exploration and Production.

   The Natural Gas Sales segment is engaged in the retail sales,           天然氣銷售板塊從事不同天然氣產品
   distribution and trading of various natural gas products. The Sales     的零售及分銷與貿易。LPG銷售板塊從
   of LPG segment is engaged in the wholesales and retail sales of         事不同LPG產品的批發與零售。LNG加
   various LPG products. LNG Processing and Terminal segment is            工與儲運板塊從事LNG加工、卸載、儲
   engaged in the processing, unloading, storage, gasification and         存、氣化及裝車。勘探與生產板塊從事
   entrucking of LNG. The Exploration and Production segment is            原油及天然氣之勘探、開發、生產和銷
   engaged in the exploration, development, production and sales of        售。
   crude oil and natural gas.

   The Group’s most senior executive management and Executive              本集團最高管理層及執行董事根據各板
   Directors assess the performance of the operating segments              塊之除所得稅費用、應佔聯營公司及合
   based on each segment’s profit/(loss) before income tax expense,        資企業之溢利減虧損前之溢利╱（虧損）
   and share of profits less losses of associates and joint ventures       評估經營板塊之表現（
                                                                                    「板塊業績」
                                                                                         ）。
   (“segment results”).

   Segment assets exclude deferred and current taxes, other                板塊資產不包括遞延及即期稅項、其他
   financial assets, investments in associates and joint ventures.         金融資產、於聯營公司及合資企業之投
                                                                           資。

   Corporate results, net, mainly refers to interest income earned         公司收支淨額主要指現金及現金等值項
   from cash and cash equivalents, net exchange (losses)/gains,            目所賺取的利息收入、匯兌（虧損）╱收
   general and administrative expenses and interest expenses               益淨額、公司層面產生之一般性和管理
   incurred at corporate level.                                            費用及利息支出。

   held at corporate level.                                                金及現金等值項目。

40 SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                           40 主要會計政策
      (a) Subsidiaries and non-controlling interests                           (a) 附 屬 公 司 及 非 控 制 性權
                                                                                   益
            Subsidiaries are entities controlled by the Group. The                附屬公司為由本集團控制之實
            Group controls an entity when it is exposed, or has rights,           體。當本集團因參與該實體之營
            to variable returns from its involvement with the entity and          運而獲得或有權享有其可變回
            has the ability to affect those returns through its power over        報，並能夠運用其對該實體之權
            the entity. When assessing whether the Group has power,               力影響上述回報，本集團即對該
            only substantive rights (held by the Group and other parties)         實體擁有控制權。評估本集團是
            are considered.                                                       否有權力時，僅考慮（本集團及其
                                                                                  他方持有的）實質權利。

            Subsidiaries are consolidated into the consolidated financial         於附屬公司的投資自控制權開始
            statements from the date that control commences until the             當日起至控制權終止當日止綜合
            date that control ceases. Intra-group balances, transactions          計入綜合財務報表。集團內部結
            and cash flows and any unrealised profits arising from                餘、交易及現金流量以及集團內
            intra- group transactions are eliminated in full in preparing         部交易所產生之任何未變現溢利
            the consolidated financial statements. Unrealised losses              於編製綜合財務報表時悉數對
            resulting from intra-group transactions are eliminated in the         銷。集團內部交易所產生之未變
            same way as unrealised gains but only to the extent that              現虧損按與未變現收益相同的方
            there is no evidence of impairment.                                   式對銷，但只限於沒有證據顯示
                                                                                  減值的情況。

            Accounting policies of subsidiaries have been changed                 附屬公司的會計政策已作出必要
            where necessary to ensure consistency with the policies               變動，以確保與本集團採納的政
            adopted by the Group.                                                 策一致。

            Non-controlling interests are presented in the consolidated           非控制性權益在綜合財務狀況表
            statement of financial position within equity, separately             於權益內與本公司權益股東應佔
            from equity attributable to the equity shareholders of the            權益分開呈列。本集團業績中之
            Company. Non-controlling interests in the results of the              非控制性權益於綜合全面收益表
            Group are presented on the face of the consolidated                   內呈列，作為該年度損益總額及
            statement of comprehensive income as an allocation of                 全面收益總額於本公司非控制性
            the total profit or loss and total comprehensive income for           權益與權益股東之間之分配。
            the year between non-controlling interests and the equity
            shareholders of the Company.

            Changes in the Group’s interests in a subsidiary that                 本集團於附屬公司權益之變動，
            do not result in a loss of control are accounted for as               如無導致失去控制權，則入賬列
            equity transactions, whereby adjustments are made to                  為股本交易，而綜合權益內的控
            the amounts of controlling and non-controlling interests              股及非控制性權益金額則予以調
            within consolidated equity to reflect the change in relative          整以反映相關權益變動，但並不
            interests, but no adjustments are made to goodwill and no             調整商譽，且不確認任何收益或
            gain or loss is recognised.                                           虧損。

40 SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES (CONTINUED)                           40 主要會計政策（續）
   (a) Subsidiaries and non-controlling interests                          (a) 附 屬 公 司 及 非 控 制性權
       (Continued)                                                             益（續）
       When the Group loses control of a subsidiary, it is                    當集團失去對附屬公司的控制權
       accounted for as a disposal of the entire interest in that             時，則入賬為出售該附屬公司全
       subsidiary, with a resulting gain or loss being recognised in          部權益，因此而產生的收益或虧
       profit or loss. Any interest retained in that former subsidiary        損於損益確認。失去控制權當日
       at the date when control is lost is recognised at fair value           於該前附屬公司留存的任何權益
       and this amount is regarded as the fair value on initial               按公平值確認且該金額視為初步
       recognition of a financial asset (see Note 40(g)) or, when             確認金融資產（見附註40(g)）時的
       appropriate, the cost on initial recognition of an investment          公平值或（倘適用）初步確認於聯
       in an associate or a joint venture (see Note 40(b)).                   營公司或合資企業（見附註40(b)）
                                                                              投資的成本。

       In the Company’s statement of financial position, an                  在本公司之財務狀況表內，於附
       investment in subsidiary is stated at cost less impairment            屬公司之投資以成本減減值虧損
       losses (see Note 40(l)(ii)).                                          （見附註40(l)(ii)）列賬。

   (b) Associates and joint ventures                                       (b) 聯營公司及合資企業
       An associate is an entity in which the Group has significant           聯營公司指本集團對其有重大影
       influence, but not control or joint control.                           響力，但無法控制或共同控制之
                                                                              實體。

       A joint venture is an arrangement whereby the Group                    合資企業為一項安排，據此本集
       and other parties contractually agree to share control of              團及其他訂約方同意共同控制該
       the arrangement, and have rights to the net assets of the              安排，及對該安排之淨資產享有
       arrangement.                                                           權利。

       An investment in an associate or a joint venture is                    聯營公司或合資企業之投資乃根
       accounted for in the consolidated financial statements                 據權益法在綜合財務報表中列
       using the equity method. Under the equity method, the                  賬。根據權益法，投資初始以成
       investment is initially recorded at cost, which includes               本記賬，成本包括購買價、直接
       purchase price, other costs directly attributable to the               來自收購該投資的其他成本，及
       acquisition of the investment, and any direct investment into          構成本集團股本投資一部分的於
       the associate or joint venture that forms part of the Group’s          聯營公司或合資企業的任何直接
       equity investment. Thereafter, the investment is adjusted              投資。其後，該投資經本集團在
       for the post acquisition change in the Group’s share of the            收購後應佔被投資單位之淨資產
       investee’s net assets and any impairment loss relating to              及與該投資有關之任何減值虧損

   (p) Taxation                                                            (p) 稅項
       Income tax expense for the period comprises current and               期間所得稅費用包括當期及遞延
       deferred tax. Tax is recognised in the profit or loss, except         稅項。稅項於損益內確認，惟如

       where the Group, associates and joint ventures operate                日已實行或大致實行之稅法計
       and generate taxable income. Management periodically                  算。管理層就適用稅務法例詮釋
       evaluates positions taken in tax returns with respect to              所規限之情況定期評估報稅表之

       interpretation. It establishes provisions where appropriate           須向稅務機關支付之稅款確定撥
       on the basis of amounts expected to be paid to the tax                備。
       authorities.

       method, on temporary differences arising between the tax              負債之稅基與彼等於綜合財務報
       bases of assets and liabilities and their carrying amounts in         表之賬面值兩者之暫時差異予以
